### `AlertDialogContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/alert-dialog-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface AlertDialogContractProps extends OverlayContractProps, FeedbackContractProps {
    readonly title: ReactNode;
    readonly description?: ReactNode;
    readonly children?: ReactNode;
    readonly confirmLabel: string;
    readonly cancelLabel: string;
    readonly isConfirmDisabled?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly onConfirm?: (() => void) | undefined;
    readonly onCancel?: (() => void) | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by the web and native `AlertDialog`. Unlike the other overlays, `isDismissable` defaults to false here
(a stray press outside must not decide a question) and Escape, or the Android back button, refuses the question
unless `isKeyboardDismissDisabled` is set.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly title: ReactNode;` | The question, required: it names the dialog for assistive technology, so word it as a decision. |
| `readonly description?: ReactNode;` | What the decision does, read out with the dialog; text, because it is what the dialog is described by. |
| `readonly children?: ReactNode;` | Anything more the decision needs, shown under the description: a field to type a name into, say. |
| `readonly confirmLabel: string;` | The label of the button that agrees, in the app's language, for example "Delete project". |
| `readonly cancelLabel: string;` | The label of the button that refuses, in the app's language, for example "Keep project". On web it takes focus first; on native it comes first in reading order. |
| `readonly isConfirmDisabled?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to refuse the confirming press until the decision is ready, as when the user must still type a name. |
| `readonly onConfirm?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the user confirms, just before the dialog closes. |
| `readonly onCancel?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the user refuses: the cancel button, Escape or the Android back button, or a press outside. |

| `readonly onPress?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the button is pressed, by pointer, touch or keyboard. |

### `CartesianChartContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/cartesian-chart-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface CartesianChartContractProps extends ChartContractProps {
    readonly xKey: string;
    readonly xFormat?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions | undefined;
    readonly stacked?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isNormalized?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly showGrid?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly showValueAxis?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly references?: readonly ChartReference[] | undefined;
    readonly showComparison?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly showBrush?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly annotations?: readonly ChartAnnotation[] | undefined;
    readonly secondaryKeys?: readonly string[] | undefined;
    readonly valueScale?: "linear" | "log" | undefined;
    readonly syncId?: string | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by the area, bar and line charts.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly xKey: string;` | The key of each row's category: a month name, an ISO date. |
| `readonly xFormat?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions \| undefined;` | Writes the category as a date in the locale (`{ month: "short" }`); categories are shown as given without it. |
| `readonly stacked?: boolean \| undefined;` | Stacks the series on one another; side by side (bars) or overlapping (areas, lines) by default. |
| `readonly isNormalized?: boolean \| undefined;` | With `stacked`, stacks each category to 100%: every series is drawn as its share of the category's total and the value axis reads in percent (area and bar charts). The tooltip and data table keep the real values. |
| `readonly showGrid?: boolean \| undefined;` | Draws the horizontal grid lines; on by default. |
| `readonly showValueAxis?: boolean \| undefined;` | Draws the value axis; off by default, since the tooltip and the data table carry the values. |
| `readonly references?: readonly ChartReference[] \| undefined;` | Target lines and threshold bands on the value axis, drawn behind the series. |
| `readonly showComparison?: boolean \| undefined;` | Draws each series' `compareKey` values as a dashed twin: the previous period beside this one. Off by default. |
| `readonly showBrush?: boolean \| undefined;` | Adds a brush under the plot to zoom into a span of the categories (web line, area and composed charts; native reads every row and ignores it). Off by default. |
| `readonly annotations?: readonly ChartAnnotation[] \| undefined;` | Notes pinned to categories (a launch, an outage): a dashed guide through each with its label at the top. |
| `readonly secondaryKeys?: readonly string[] \| undefined;` | The keys of the series read against a second value axis at the reading end (the left in Arabic), for two measures of different size on one chart (orders and revenue). Vertical charts only; references stay on the first axis. |
| `readonly valueScale?: "linear" \| "log" \| undefined;` | The value axis' scale: `linear` (the default) or `log` for values that span several powers of ten. On a log scale a value of zero or below is not drawn; use it on lines and unstacked charts. |
| `readonly syncId?: string \| undefined;` | Charts that share a `syncId` point at the same row together: pointing one moves the others' crosshair and tooltip (web; native reads one chart at a time and ignores it). |

A setting of another kind is ignored. |
| `readonly aggregate?: AggregateKind \| undefined;` | The aggregate the footer shows for the column at first. |
| `readonly isEditable?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to let a DataTable edit the cell in place (with `onCellEdit`). A DataGrid edits every column not read-only. |
| `readonly isReadOnly?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to refuse edits in this column everywhere; the first column of a DataGrid matrix is always read-only. |
| `readonly isRequired?: boolean \| undefined;` | True when an empty cell is an error. |
| `readonly min?: number \| undefined;` | The least value of a `number` column. |
| `readonly max?: number \| undefined;` | The greatest value of a `number` column. |
| `readonly validate?: ((value: DataGridValue, row: DataGridRow) => string \| undefined) \| undefined;` | The app's own rule: a message in the active locale for a bad value, or undefined. |

### `CollectionController`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/collection-controller.type.ts`

```ts
interface CollectionController<TItem extends CollectionItem = CollectionItem> {
    readonly collectionKey: string | undefined;
    readonly mode: CollectionMode;
    readonly paging: CollectionPaging;
    readonly state: CollectionState;
    readonly columns: readonly ResolvedCollectionColumn[];
    readonly visibleColumns: readonly ResolvedCollectionColumn[];
    readonly rows: readonly TItem[];
    readonly groups: readonly CollectionGroup<TItem>[] | null;
    readonly totalCount: number;
    readonly pageCount: number;
    readonly hasMore: boolean;
    readonly aggregates: Readonly<Record<string, number | null>>;
    readonly selected: CollectionSelection;
    readonly isLoading: boolean;
    readonly error: unknown;
    readonly query: CollectionQuery;
    readonly isFiltered: boolean;
    readonly views: readonly CollectionView[];
    readonly defaultViewId: string | null;
    readonly update: (patch: Partial<CollectionState>) => void;
    readonly setSearch: (search: string) => void;
    readonly setFilter: (filter: CollectionFilter) => void;
    readonly setSort: (sort: readonly SortDescriptor[]) => void;
    readonly toggleSort: (columnId: string, isAdditive?: boolean) => void;
    readonly setGroupBy: (columnId: string | null) => void;
    readonly setAggregate: (columnId: string, kind: AggregateKind | null) => void;
    readonly moveColumn: (columnId: string, toIndex: number) => void;
    readonly reorderColumns: (columnIds: readonly string[]) => void;
    readonly updateColumn: (columnId: string, patch: Omit<CollectionColumnState, "id">) => void;
    readonly resetColumns: () => void;
    readonly setDensity: (density: CollectionDensity) => void;
    readonly setLayout: (layout: CollectionLayout) => void;
    readonly setSelection: (keys: readonly string[]) => void;
    readonly setAllMatchingSelected: (isAll: boolean) => void;
    readonly setExcludedKeys: (keys: readonly string[]) => void;
    readonly setPage: (page: number) => void;
    readonly setPageSize: (pageSize: number) => void;
    readonly loadMore: () => void;
    readonly applyView: (viewId: string) => void;
    readonly saveViewAs: (label: string) => string;
    readonly saveView: (viewId: string) => void;
    readonly renameView: (viewId: string, label: string) => void;
    readonly deleteView: (viewId: string) => void;
    readonly setDefaultView: (viewId: string | null) => void;
    readonly reset: () => void;
    readonly refresh: () => void;
}
```

A collection's state and the changes it takes.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly collectionKey: string \| undefined;` | The key the state persists under, if any. |
| `readonly mode: CollectionMode;` | `client` (the engine searches, filters, sorts and pages) or `server` (the app does, from `query`). |
| `readonly paging: CollectionPaging;` | How the rows are paged. |
| `readonly state: CollectionState;` | The whole state. |
| `readonly columns: readonly ResolvedCollectionColumn[];` | Every column with the user's state applied, in display order: pinned start, the rest, pinned end. |
| `readonly visibleColumns: readonly ResolvedCollectionColumn[];` | The columns not hidden, in display order. |
| `readonly rows: readonly TItem[];` | The rows to draw: in client mode searched, filtered, sorted and paged; in server mode as the app gave them. |
| `readonly groups: readonly CollectionGroup<TItem>[] \| null;` | The rows gathered by `state.groupBy`, or `null` when not grouped. |
| `readonly totalCount: number;` | How many rows match the query in all. |
| `readonly pageCount: number;` | How many pages there are (`page` paging); 1 otherwise. |
| `readonly hasMore: boolean;` | True while there are rows after the ones drawn. |
| `readonly aggregates: Readonly<Record<string, number \| null>>;` | The footer aggregate of each column in `state.aggregates`, over every matching row. |
| `readonly selected: CollectionSelection;` | What a bulk action acts on: the selected ids; with "all matching" chosen, every matching id in client mode, or in server mode a descriptor of the search and filter (never ids the client does not hold). |
| `readonly isLoading: boolean;` | True while a server page loads. |
| `readonly error: unknown;` | What the last server fetch threw, or `undefined`. |
| `readonly query: CollectionQuery;` | The query of the state, for a server. |
| `readonly isFiltered: boolean;` | True when the search or the filter narrows the rows. |
| `readonly views: readonly CollectionView[];` | The saved views, in order. |
| `readonly defaultViewId: string \| null;` | The view applied when the collection opens, or `null`. |
| `readonly update: (patch: Partial<CollectionState>) => void;` | Merges a part of the state in. |
| `readonly setSearch: (search: string) => void;` | Sets the quick search, back on the first page. |
| `readonly setFilter: (filter: CollectionFilter) => void;` | Sets the rule tree, back on the first page. |
| `readonly setSort: (sort: readonly SortDescriptor[]) => void;` | Sets the sort keys. |
| `readonly toggleSort: (columnId: string, isAdditive?: boolean) => void;` | Sorts by a column: ascending, then descending, then off. Additive, it adds or turns the column's key and keeps the others (multi-sort); otherwise the column becomes the only key. |
| `readonly setGroupBy: (columnId: string \| null) => void;` | Groups the rows by a column, or stops grouping with `null`. |
| `readonly setAggregate: (columnId: string, kind: AggregateKind \| null) => void;` | Sets the footer aggregate of a column, or clears it with `null`. |
| `readonly moveColumn: (columnId: string, toIndex: number) => void;` | Moves a column to a display index. |
| `readonly reorderColumns: (columnIds: readonly string[]) => void;` | Puts the columns in this order. |
| `readonly updateColumn: (columnId: string, patch: Omit<CollectionColumnState, "id">) => void;` | Changes one column: hidden, name, width, pin or wrap. |
| `readonly resetColumns: () => void;` | Puts every column back as defined. |
| `readonly setDensity: (density: CollectionDensity) => void;` | Sets the density. |
| `readonly setLayout: (layout: CollectionLayout) => void;` | Sets the view the rows are drawn as. |
| `readonly setSelection: (keys: readonly string[]) => void;` | Sets the selected row ids; any change drops "all matching". |
| `readonly setAllMatchingSelected: (isAll: boolean) => void;` | Selects every matching row beyond the ones loaded, or goes back to the rows selected; either clears the exclusions. |
| `readonly setExcludedKeys: (keys: readonly string[]) => void;` | With "all matching" chosen, the rows unticked since: every matching row but these stays selected. |
| `readonly setPage: (page: number) => void;` | Shows a page, from 0. |
| `readonly setPageSize: (pageSize: number) => void;` | Sets the rows per page, back on the first page. |
| `readonly loadMore: () => void;` | Loads the next page: appended for `infinite`, the next cursor for `cursor`. |
| `readonly applyView: (viewId: string) => void;` | Applies a saved view over the defaults. |
| `readonly saveViewAs: (label: string) => string;` | Saves the current state as a new view and applies it; returns its id. |
| `readonly saveView: (viewId: string) => void;` | Saves the current state over an existing view. |
| `readonly renameView: (viewId: string, label: string) => void;` | Renames a view. |
| `readonly deleteView: (viewId: string) => void;` | Deletes a view. |
| `readonly setDefaultView: (viewId: string \| null) => void;` | Makes a view the one applied on open, or none with `null`. |
| `readonly reset: () => void;` | Puts the whole state back as first given (the saved views stay). |
| `readonly refresh: () => void;` | Fetches the page again (server mode with a fetcher). |

### `ComboBoxBaseContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/combo-box-base-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface ComboBoxBaseContractProps {
    readonly items: readonly SelectItem[];
    readonly placeholder?: string | undefined;
    readonly isFullWidth?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly inputValue?: string | undefined;
    readonly onInputChange?: ((text: string) => void) | undefined;
    readonly isServerFiltered?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isLoading?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly loadingLabel?: string | undefined;
    readonly onLoadMore?: (() => void) | undefined;
    readonly emptyMessage?: ReactNode;
}
```

The props of a ComboBox in either mode.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly items: readonly SelectItem[];` | The choices that match the text; with `isServerFiltered` the app narrows them as the text changes. |
| `readonly placeholder?: string \| undefined;` | Text shown while the field is empty; it hints at what to pick and never replaces the label. |
| `readonly isFullWidth?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to fill the width of the parent instead of the width of the content. |
| `readonly inputValue?: string \| undefined;` | The text typed to filter the list, for a controlled search box; pair it with `onInputChange`. |
| `readonly onInputChange?: ((text: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the text each time the user types, which is where an async ComboBox starts its search. |
| `readonly isServerFiltered?: boolean \| undefined;` | True when the app has already narrowed `items` to the text, so the field shows them all and filters nothing. |
| `readonly isLoading?: boolean \| undefined;` | True while items load: a spinner shows under the list. |
| `readonly loadingLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken name of the spinner while items load, for example "Loading more countries"; without it the spinner is silent. |
| `readonly onLoadMore?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the user scrolls to the end of the list, to fetch the next page of choices. |
| `readonly emptyMessage?: ReactNode;` | What the list says when no choice matches, for example "No results"; without it the list stays closed. |

type ConfirmFunction = (options: ConfirmationOptions) => Promise<boolean>;

| `readonly size?: Size \| undefined;` | The height and text size of the buttons. Defaults to `md`. |

| `readonly previousLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The text of the previous button. Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's word in the active locale. |
| `readonly nextLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The text of the next button. Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's word in the active locale. |
| `readonly loadMoreLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The text of the load-more button. Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's phrase in the active locale. |
| `readonly countLabel?: ((count: number) => string) \| undefined;` | Writes the count as a sentence, for example "Showing 20 orders". Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's phrase in the active locale. |

| ------ | ----------- |

| `readonly collection?: CollectionController<TRow> \| undefined;` | The engine to draw from (`useCollection`): its rows, sort, filter, density and columns as arranged. |
| `readonly items?: readonly TRow[] \| undefined;` | Shorthand without `collection`: the saved rows of a client-mode engine the grid runs itself. |
| `readonly columns?: readonly CollectionColumn[] \| undefined;` | Shorthand without `collection`: the columns in reading order; the first names each row. |
| `readonly collectionKey?: string \| undefined;` | The key the grid's own engine keeps the user's column layout under; left out, nothing persists. |
| `readonly onSave?: ((changes: DataGridChanges) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined;` | Saves the pending changes; a returned promise keeps Save busy, and the changes clear once it resolves. |
| `readonly onChangesChange?: ((changes: DataGridChanges) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the pending changes each time they change, for example to warn before leaving. |
| `readonly isReadOnly?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to show the values without letting them be edited. |
| `readonly variant?: DataGridVariant \| undefined;` | `sheet`, the default, or `matrix`: number cells with a total per row and per column. |
| `readonly rowHeight?: number \| undefined;` | Web: the height of a row in CSS pixels; rows are virtualised at this height. Defaults to the engine's density. |
| `readonly maxHeight?: string \| undefined;` | Web: the height the rows scroll inside, as a CSS length. Defaults to `24rem`. |
| `readonly hasColumnTools?: boolean \| undefined;` | Web: true to give each header a resize handle (drag it, or focus it and use the arrows, Home and End), drag to reorder, and the column menu. Defaults to false. |

| `readonly onFilterColumn?: ((columnId: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called by a column menu's "Filter by this column"; left out, the menu has no such item. |

| `readonly onMotionChange?: ((motion: MotionLevel) => void) \| undefined;` | Called when `setMotion` picks a level. |
| `readonly accessibility?: AccessibilityPreferences \| undefined;` | The accessibility modes the user turned on in the app (ADR 0501): more contrast, reduced transparency, a web text scale and the colour-blind-safe chart palette. The web provider sets them on `<html>`; the system settings apply as well, whatever is passed here. Native reads Dynamic Type and the system settings itself. |
| `readonly direction?: Direction \| undefined;` | The text direction; derived from the locale when left out. |
| `readonly theme?: string \| undefined;` | The name of the theme whose stylesheet the app loads. Defaults to `stackra`. |
| `readonly material?: string \| undefined;` | The mode of the theme's glass material, a name its stylesheet knows (`solid`, `frosted`, `glass` or `liquid` for the `@stackra/tokens` themes), set on `<html data-material>`. Left out, the provider leaves the attribute alone and the theme's own `materials.defaultMode` applies (the stackra theme: `solid`, so product UI is opaque until it opts in; ADR 0320 decision 7 as amended 2026-10-31). |
| `readonly family?: string \| undefined;` | The theme family, `front` (storefront, marketing, consumer mobile: glass chrome and surfaces) or `studio` (dashboard: glass only on overlays and floating chrome), set on `<html data-family>`. Left out, the theme's own `materials.family` applies (the stackra theme: `studio`). |
| `readonly maxFontScale?: number \| null \| undefined;` | The largest text scale native text renders at, for dense screens (a till, a kitchen display); leave it out, or pass `null`, to follow the device's Dynamic Type or font scale with no cap. Text always scales with the device setting up to this cap, and there is no in-app text-size setting. Native only: the web follows the browser's own zoom and text size. |
| `readonly shortcuts?: ShortcutSettings \| undefined;` | The shortcut settings in force (`resolveShortcutSettings` of the platform, tenant and person layers): on or off, the hint mode, single-key shortcuts and remaps. Left out, the provider holds the platform default. Web only today. |
| `readonly onShortcutsChange?: ((settings: ShortcutSettings) => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the person changes a shortcut setting (the settings panel); persist the person's layer here. |
| `readonly canRunShortcut?: ((permission: string) => boolean) \| undefined;` | Whether the person holds a permission; an action whose `permission` it refuses never registers. |
| `readonly hasShortcutCheatSheet?: boolean \| undefined;` | False leaves out the built-in shortcuts cheat sheet (`?`). Defaults to true. |

The props of an EntityPicker in either mode.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly loadOptions: (search: string, cursor?: string) => Promise<EntityOptionPage>;` | Answers a search: the page of records that match `search` (an empty string asks for the first records), or the page after `cursor`. A picker asks again when the user pauses typing and ignores an answer that a later request has replaced. A rejection shows `loadErrorMessage` in the list; typing again asks again. |
| `readonly placeholder?: string \| undefined;` | Text shown while the field is empty; it hints at what to pick and never replaces the label. |
| `readonly isFullWidth?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to fill the width of the parent instead of a fixed field width. |
| `readonly debounceMs?: number \| undefined;` | How long, in milliseconds, the user must stop typing before a search starts. Defaults to 250. |
| `readonly loadingLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken name of the spinner while records load, for example "Loading people"; without it the spinner is silent. |
| `readonly emptyMessage?: ReactNode;` | What the list says when no record matches, for example "No people found"; without it the list stays closed. |
| `readonly loadErrorMessage?: ReactNode;` | What the list says when `loadOptions` rejects, for example "People could not be loaded. Type to try again." |
| `readonly onCreate?: ((text: string) => EntityOption \| void \| Promise<EntityOption \| void>) \| undefined;` | Called with the typed text when the user picks the create-new row. It may return the new record, which is then picked, or nothing, when the app handles the result itself. A rejection picks nothing, and the app reports it (ADR 0139). The row shows only when `createLabel` is also given. |
| `readonly createLabel?: ((text: string) => string) \| undefined;` | The text of the create-new row from the typed text, for example `(text) => \`Add "$\{text\}"\``. |

Call `progress` with the share done, from 0 to 1, to fill the bar; never called, the bar stays indeterminate. |

### `FieldContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/field-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface FieldContractProps<TValue> {
    readonly label?: ReactNode;
    readonly description?: ReactNode;
    readonly errorMessage?: ReactNode;
    readonly isRequired?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isInvalid?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isDisabled?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isReadOnly?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly value?: TValue | undefined;
    readonly defaultValue?: TValue | undefined;
    readonly onChange?: ((value: TValue) => void) | undefined;
    readonly name?: string | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by every field on web and native.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly label?: ReactNode;` | The visible label; it is the field's accessible name, so a field without one needs the runtime's own name prop. |
| `readonly description?: ReactNode;` | Help text under the field, tied to it for assistive technology. |
| `readonly errorMessage?: ReactNode;` | What is wrong, shown under the field while `isInvalid` is true, and read out with the field. |
| `readonly isRequired?: boolean \| undefined;` | True when the form cannot be submitted without a value; the label shows it and assistive technology is told. |
| `readonly isInvalid?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to show the error state; the field alone never decides, so a form adapter sets it from its validation. |
| `readonly isDisabled?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to refuse input and show the disabled style; the field leaves the tab order and is left out of the submit. |
| `readonly isReadOnly?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to show the value but refuse changes; unlike disabled, the field keeps focus and is submitted. |
| `readonly value?: TValue \| undefined;` | The current value, for a controlled field; pair it with `onChange`. |
| `readonly defaultValue?: TValue \| undefined;` | The starting value, for an uncontrolled field. |
| `readonly onChange?: ((value: TValue) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the new value each time the user changes it. |
| `readonly name?: string \| undefined;` | The name the value is submitted under in a form, and the key a form adapter binds. |

| `readonly onMarkAllRead?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called by the mark-all-read button, which shows only with it and while something is unread. |
| `readonly label?: string \| undefined;` | The bell's accessible name; defaults to "Notifications" with the unread count. |
| `readonly title?: string \| undefined;` | The panel's heading; defaults to "Notifications". |
| `readonly emptyState?: ReactNode \| undefined;` | What an empty panel shows; defaults to "You're all caught up". |
| `readonly footer?: ReactNode \| undefined;` | Content under the list, such as a "View all" link. |
| `readonly isInline?: boolean \| undefined;` | Shows the inbox in place, as a card under its heading, for a notifications page or a dashboard tile: no bell, no popover or drawer, and the open state is ignored. Defaults to `false`. |

### `NumberFieldContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/number-field-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface NumberFieldContractProps extends FieldContractProps<number | null> {
    readonly placeholder?: string | undefined;
    readonly variant?: TextFieldVariant | undefined;
    readonly isFullWidth?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly minValue?: number | undefined;
    readonly maxValue?: number | undefined;

    readonly formatOptions?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions | undefined;
    readonly hasSteppers?: boolean | undefined;

    readonly decrementLabel?: string | undefined;

```

Props shared by the web and native `NumberField`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly placeholder?: string \| undefined;` | Hint text drawn inside the empty input. It never replaces the label: it is gone once the user types. |
| `readonly variant?: TextFieldVariant \| undefined;` | The look of the field: `primary` on the page, `secondary` on a tinted surface. Defaults to `primary`. |
| `readonly isFullWidth?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to fill the parent's width, false to size to the content. Defaults to true. |
| `readonly minValue?: number \| undefined;` | The smallest value; the field clamps a typed or stepped value to it, and the decrement button stops there. |
| `readonly maxValue?: number \| undefined;` | The largest value; the field clamps a typed or stepped value to it, and the increment button stops there. |
| `readonly step?: number \| undefined;` | How far one press of a stepper button, or an arrow key, moves the value. Defaults to 1. |
| `readonly formatOptions?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions \| undefined;` | How the number is written and read: percent, currency, unit, fraction digits and grouping, as `Intl` takes them. |
| `readonly hasSteppers?: boolean \| undefined;` | False to hide the increment and decrement buttons and keep the arrow keys. Defaults to true. |
| `readonly incrementLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken name of the increment button, for example "Add one seat". Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's word in the active locale. |
| `readonly decrementLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken name of the decrement button, for example "Remove one seat". Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's word in the active locale. |

### `ProgressContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/progress-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface ProgressContractProps {
    readonly value?: number | undefined;

    readonly maxValue?: number | undefined;

    readonly tone?: Tone | undefined;
    readonly label: string;

}
```

Props shared by every progress indicator on web and native.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly value?: number \| undefined;` | How far along it is, from `minValue` to `maxValue`; a number outside the range is held to its nearest end. Left out, the indicator is indeterminate: the work is under way and its end is unknown. |
| `readonly minValue?: number \| undefined;` | The value that means nothing done yet. Defaults to 0. |
| `readonly maxValue?: number \| undefined;` | The value that means done. Defaults to 100. |
| `readonly size?: Size \| undefined;` | The thickness of a bar, or the diameter of a circle. Defaults to `md`. |
| `readonly tone?: Tone \| undefined;` | The colour role of the fill; `default` is the foreground colour. Defaults to `accent`, as in HeroUI. |
| `readonly label: string;` | The accessible name, for example "Uploading invoices". Required: an unnamed progress bar is a barrier for a screen reader user. A bar can also show it, see `isLabelVisible`. |
| `readonly valueLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken text of the value, for example "3 of 12 files", in place of the percentage. Left out, the percentage in the reader's locale is spoken. It is the shown text too when a bar's value is visible. |

Props shared by the web and native `PropertyList`.

| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly items: readonly TItem[];` | The rows, in order. |
| `readonly columns?: 1 \| 2 \| "auto" \| undefined;` | How many columns of rows to draw. Defaults to 1; on a narrow screen two columns stack into one. `auto` follows the list's own width (a container query on web): one column when narrow, two from 28rem, three from 48rem. |
| `readonly emptyValue?: ReactNode;` | What shows in place of an empty value. Defaults to an en dash; pass words ("Not set") when a dash would not say enough. |
| `readonly copiedLabel?: string \| undefined;` | Spoken, and on web also drawn as a check, after a value is copied, for example "Copied". Defaults to the `ui` catalogue's word in the active locale, so a copy is never silent. |
| `readonly onCopy?: ((item: TItem, text: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the row and its text once the text is on the clipboard. On web the list writes the clipboard itself; on native it has no clipboard module, so the app writes it here and a row has a copy button only when this is given. |

### `RecurrenceRule`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/recurrence-rule.type.ts`

```ts
interface RecurrenceRule {
    readonly frequency: "daily" | "weekly" | "monthly" | "yearly";
    readonly interval?: number | undefined;
    readonly weekdays?: readonly RecurrenceWeekday[] | undefined;
    readonly nth?: number | undefined;
    readonly monthDay?: number | undefined;
    readonly month?: number | undefined;
    readonly count?: number | undefined;
    readonly until?: string | undefined;
    readonly calendar?: CalendarSystem | undefined;
}
```

One repeating rule.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly frequency: "daily" \| "weekly" \| "monthly" \| "yearly";` | `FREQ`: the unit it repeats in. |
| `readonly interval?: number \| undefined;` | `INTERVAL`: every how many units; 1 when left out. |
| `readonly weekdays?: readonly RecurrenceWeekday[] \| undefined;` | `BYDAY`: the weekdays of a weekly rule, or the one weekday of a monthly rule with `nth`. |
| `readonly nth?: number \| undefined;` | With one weekday on a monthly or yearly rule: which one of the month, 1 to 4, or -1 for the last. |
| `readonly monthDay?: number \| undefined;` | `BYMONTHDAY`: the day of the month, 1 to 31, or -1 for the last day. |
| `readonly month?: number \| undefined;` | `BYMONTH`: the month of a yearly rule, 1 to 12, in `calendar`. |
| `readonly count?: number \| undefined;` | `COUNT`: how many times in all; the rule never ends when neither this nor `until` is set. |
| `readonly until?: string \| undefined;` | `UNTIL`: the last day it may fall on, a Gregorian ISO date (`2026-12-31`). |
| `readonly calendar?: CalendarSystem \| undefined;` | `RSCALE`: the calendar months and month days count in. Gregorian when left out. |

A promise holds the item where it was dropped, pending, until it settles; `false` puts it back. Anything else is taken as accepted: the screen passes the new items. |

Props shared by the web and native `Sheet`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly children: ReactNode;` | The sheet's parts, usually a header, a body and a footer; any element is allowed. |
| `readonly trigger?: ReactElement \| undefined;` | The element that opens the sheet when pressed: a Button, or any pressable element. Leave it out for a sheet the app opens through `isOpen`. Focus returns to it when the sheet closes. |
| `readonly placement?: Placement \| undefined;` | The edge it comes from: `bottom` (the default), `top`, or `start` and `end` by reading direction. |
| `readonly snapPoints?: readonly SnapPoint[] \| undefined;` | The partial sizes it rests at, smallest first: pixels (points on native) or a share as `"50%"`. It opens at the first, a swipe moves through them (several in one swipe) and the full size is always the last stop, so it is not listed. |
| `readonly isDismissable?: boolean \| undefined;` | True, the default, to close on a press outside, Escape or a swipe away. False keeps it open until a close control inside it is pressed. |
| `readonly backdrop?: SheetBackdrop \| undefined;` | The backdrop's look: `opaque` (the default), `blur` or `transparent`. |
| `readonly isDetached?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to float the sheet clear of the screen's edges with every corner rounded. Defaults to false on web and true on native (every native sheet is inset, owner decision 2026-10-24). |
| `readonly label?: string \| undefined;` | The accessible name for a sheet that has no `Sheet.Title`; with a title, the title names it. |

interface TableColumn {

One column of a Table.

| `readonly maxWidth?: number \| undefined;` | The widest a resize may make the column, in CSS pixels. Defaults to 640. |

### `TableContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/table-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface TableContractProps<TItem extends CollectionItem = CollectionItem> extends CollectionContractProps<TItem>, SelectionContractProps {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly columns: readonly TableColumn[];
    readonly renderCell?: ((item: TItem, column: TableColumn) => ReactNode) | undefined;
    readonly variant?: TableVariant | undefined;
    readonly onSortChange?: ((sort: SortDescriptor) => void) | undefined;
    readonly hasMore?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly onLoadMore?: (() => void) | undefined;
    readonly isStickyHeader?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly maxHeight?: string | undefined;
    readonly sort?: readonly SortDescriptor[] | undefined;
    readonly density?: CollectionDensity | undefined;
    readonly isColumnResizable?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly onColumnResize?: ((columnId: string, width: number) => void) | undefined;
    readonly onColumnMove?: ((columnId: string, toIndex: number) => void) | undefined;
    readonly renderColumnMenu?: ((column: TableColumn, tools: {
        readonly startResize: () => void;
    }) => ReactNode) | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by the web and native `Table`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly label: string;` | The spoken name of the table, for example "Orders"; a table without one is anonymous to assistive technology. |
| `readonly columns: readonly TableColumn[];` | The columns, in reading order. |
| `readonly renderCell?: ((item: TItem, column: TableColumn) => ReactNode) \| undefined;` | The content of one cell. Left out, a cell shows the text of the row's property named by the column's `id`; give it to draw a badge, a date or a link, and give the app's own text for each value, as the table adds none. |
| `readonly variant?: TableVariant \| undefined;` | The look of the table. Defaults to `primary`. |
| `readonly onSortChange?: ((sort: SortDescriptor) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the sort a press on a sortable header asks for; the app fetches the rows in that order. |
| `readonly hasMore?: boolean \| undefined;` | True while a cursor page after the last row exists; with `onLoadMore` it loads it when the end comes into view. |
| `readonly onLoadMore?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when the end of the rows is reached while `hasMore` is true and nothing is loading. |
| `readonly isStickyHeader?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to keep the header in view while the rows scroll; the table then scrolls inside `maxHeight`. |
| `readonly maxHeight?: string \| undefined;` | The height the rows scroll inside when the header is sticky, as a CSS length. Defaults to `24rem`. |
| `readonly sort?: readonly SortDescriptor[] \| undefined;` | The sort in force, every key most significant first, for a controlled table: the rows arrive sorted, and this only marks the headers (the first by its direction, the others by their rank too). |
| `readonly density?: CollectionDensity \| undefined;` | How tightly the rows are drawn: `compact`, `normal` (the default) or `roomy`. Native reads it as nothing. |
| `readonly isColumnResizable?: boolean \| undefined;` | Web: true to give each header a resize handle (pointer, and the keyboard through the column menu's "Resize"); the columns then take their `width`, `minWidth` and `maxWidth`, and the table scrolls sideways past its width. |
| `readonly onColumnResize?: ((columnId: string, width: number) => void) \| undefined;` | Web: called with a column's new width, in CSS pixels, when a resize ends. |
| `readonly onColumnMove?: ((columnId: string, toIndex: number) => void) \| undefined;` | Web: given, headers can be dragged to reorder the columns; called with the column and its new index. |
| `readonly renderColumnMenu?: ((column: TableColumn, tools: { readonly startResize: () => void; }) => ReactNode) \| undefined;` | Web: what sits at the end of a header, usually the column's menu. `startResize` starts a keyboard resize of the column (arrows, then Enter or Escape) when the table is resizable. |

### `TabsContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/tabs-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface TabsContractProps extends CollectionContractProps<TabItem>, Omit<SelectionContractProps, "selectionMode"> {
    readonly label?: string | undefined;
    readonly orientation?: Orientation | undefined;
    readonly variant?: Exclude<Variant, "tertiary"> | "steps" | undefined;
    readonly isDisabled?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly overflow?: "scroll" | "menu" | undefined;
    readonly onClose?: ((id: string) => void) | undefined;
    readonly hasNumberShortcuts?: boolean | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by the web and native `Tabs`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly label?: string \| undefined;` | The spoken name of the tab list, for example "Order sections"; name it when a page holds more than one. |
| `readonly orientation?: Orientation \| undefined;` | The axis the tabs run along. Defaults to `horizontal`; native has no vertical tabs and reads it as horizontal. |
| `readonly variant?: Exclude<Variant, "tertiary"> \| "steps" \| undefined;` | The emphasis of the selected tab: `primary` is a raised pill, `secondary` a flat one, and `steps` plain words joined by arrows with the selected step in the accent (a marketing switcher, such as Action → Endpoint → Client; native draws it as `secondary`). Defaults to `primary`. |
| `readonly isDisabled?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to show every tab but refuse selecting any of them. |
| `readonly overflow?: "scroll" \| "menu" \| undefined;` | What happens to tabs that do not fit the row: `scroll` (the default) scrolls the row; `menu` keeps the tabs that fit and lists the rest in a "More" menu at the end (the selected tab always stays in the row). Native always scrolls. |
| `readonly onClose?: ((id: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with a closable tab's id when its close mark is pressed, or Delete is pressed on it on web. |
| `readonly hasNumberShortcuts?: boolean \| undefined;` | True binds Mod+1 to Mod+9 to the first nine usable tabs while the page has no overlay open (ADR 0508). Off by default: browsers use Mod+1 to Mod+9 to switch their own tabs, and a page with several tab rows would bind them all; turn it on for the one main tab row of a page. Native ignores it. |

### `TamoTasks`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/tamo-tasks.type.ts`

```ts
interface TamoTasks {
    readonly transform: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly text: string;
            readonly action: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly field?: string | undefined;
        };
        readonly output: string;
    };
    readonly continue: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly text: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly field?: string | undefined;
        };
        readonly output: string;
    };
    readonly draft: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly prompt: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly field?: string | undefined;
        };
        readonly output: string;
    };
    readonly filter: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly text: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly columns: readonly TamoFieldHint[];
        };
        readonly output: {
            readonly conditions: readonly RuleCondition[];
            readonly sort: readonly SortDescriptor[];
        };
    };
    readonly summarise: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly rows: readonly Readonly<Record<string, string>>[];
            readonly locale: string;
        };
        readonly output: string;
    };
    readonly fill_column: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly column: TamoFieldHint;
            readonly instruction: string;
            readonly rows: readonly {
                readonly id: string;
                readonly values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
            }[];
            readonly locale: string;
        };
        readonly output: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    };
    readonly fill_form: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly text: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly fields: readonly TamoFieldHint[];
        };
        readonly output: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    };
    readonly date: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly text: string;
            readonly locale: string;
            readonly today: string;
            readonly isRange: boolean;
        };
        readonly output: {
            readonly start: string;
            readonly end?: string | undefined;
        } | null;
    };
    readonly ask: {
        readonly input: {
            readonly question: string;
            readonly locale: string;
        };
        readonly output: string;
    };
}
```

The Tamo tasks: `input` is what a part sends, `output` what the AI module answers.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly transform: { readonly input: { readonly text: string; readonly action: string; readonly locale: string; readonly field?: string \| undefined; }; readonly output: string; };` | A text action (a `TamoActionKind` or a contributed action's id) on the text; the new text. |
| `readonly continue: { readonly input: { readonly text: string; readonly locale: string; readonly field?: string \| undefined; }; readonly output: string; };` | What comes next after the text, shown as ghost text. |
| `readonly draft: { readonly input: { readonly prompt: string; readonly locale: string; readonly field?: string \| undefined; }; readonly output: string; };` | A first draft of an empty field from the person's words. |
| `readonly filter: { readonly input: { readonly text: string; readonly locale: string; readonly columns: readonly TamoFieldHint[]; }; readonly output: { readonly conditions: readonly RuleCondition[]; readonly sort: readonly SortDescriptor[]; }; };` | Words to filter and sort a collection: the conditions (ids `filter:<column>`) and the sort keys. |
| `readonly summarise: { readonly input: { readonly rows: readonly Readonly<Record<string, string>>[]; readonly locale: string; }; readonly output: string; };` | A short summary of rows, given as their shown text by column label. |
| `readonly fill_column: { readonly input: { readonly column: TamoFieldHint; readonly instruction: string; readonly rows: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; }[]; readonly locale: string; }; readonly output: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; };` | A value for one column on each row, by row id. |
| `readonly fill_form: { readonly input: { readonly text: string; readonly locale: string; readonly fields: readonly TamoFieldHint[]; }; readonly output: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; };` | Form values from pasted text, by field name; a field the text does not give is left out. |
| `readonly date: { readonly input: { readonly text: string; readonly locale: string; readonly today: string; readonly isRange: boolean; }; readonly output: { readonly start: string; readonly end?: string \| undefined; } \| null; };` | A day (`start`) or a range (`start` and `end`), ISO calendar days, from words; `null` when none is meant. |
| `readonly ask: { readonly input: { readonly question: string; readonly locale: string; }; readonly output: string; };` | An answer to a question about the current screen. |

Props shared by the web and native `TaskTray`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |

| `readonly onDismiss?: ((id: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with a finished task's id (done, failed or canceled) when its remove button is pressed. |
| `readonly onClear?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called when "Clear finished" is pressed; the button shows while a task has finished. |
| `readonly isCollapsed?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to fold the list to the header, for a controlled tray; pair it with `onCollapsedChange`. |
| `readonly defaultCollapsed?: boolean \| undefined;` | Folded at the start, for an uncontrolled tray. Defaults to false. |
| `readonly onCollapsedChange?: ((isCollapsed: boolean) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the new folded state when the header's toggle is pressed. |
| `readonly placement?: "docked" \| "inline" \| undefined;` | `docked` (the default) pins it to the bottom end corner of the screen; `inline` leaves it in the flow. |

### `TextTone`

Constant · declared in `src/contracts/enums/text-tone.enum.ts`

```ts
const TextTone: readonly ["default", "muted", "accent", "success", "warning", "danger"];

type TextTone = (typeof TextTone)[number];
```

The text tones. `default` and `muted` are HeroUI Typography's `color` values on both runtimes; the others are the
theme's colour utilities. Each passes WCAG AA as text on `background` and `surface` only in the stackra theme. On
`surface-secondary` and `surface-tertiary` some fall short (light `warning` is 4.26:1 on tertiary, dark `danger` is
4.19:1), so pick the tone for the surface it sits on.

Constant · declared in `src/contracts/enums/text-type.enum.ts`

```ts

type TextType = (typeof TextType)[number];
```

The text styles, largest first. Each has an Arabic twin in the theme (`body-ar`, …) that the renderers pick for
Arabic text; HeroUI v3 and HeroUI Native call them `body`, `body-sm` and `body-xs`.

### `TreeViewContractProps`

Interface · declared in `src/contracts/types/tree-view-contract-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface TreeViewContractProps extends SelectionContractProps {
    readonly items: readonly TreeViewNode[];
    readonly label: string;
    readonly expandedKeys?: readonly string[] | undefined;
    readonly defaultExpandedKeys?: readonly string[] | undefined;
    readonly onExpandedChange?: ((keys: readonly string[]) => void) | undefined;
    readonly onAction?: ((id: string) => void) | undefined;
    readonly isSelectionPropagated?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly onLoadChildren?: ((id: string) => Promise<readonly TreeViewNode[]>) | undefined;
    readonly onMove?: ((ids: readonly string[], target: TreeViewDropTarget) => void) | undefined;
}
```

Props shared by the web and native `TreeView`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly items: readonly TreeViewNode[];` | The top-level rows, in order. |
| `readonly label: string;` | The accessible name of the tree, for example "Chart of accounts". |
| `readonly expandedKeys?: readonly string[] \| undefined;` | The ids of the expanded rows, for a controlled tree; pair it with `onExpandedChange`. |
| `readonly defaultExpandedKeys?: readonly string[] \| undefined;` | The ids expanded at the start, for an uncontrolled tree. |
| `readonly onExpandedChange?: ((keys: readonly string[]) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the ids now expanded each time a row opens or closes. |
| `readonly onAction?: ((id: string) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with a row's id when it is activated (pressed, or Enter on web). |
| `readonly isSelectionPropagated?: boolean \| undefined;` | True with `selectionMode="multiple"`: checking a row checks every row under it, a row is checked once all its rows are, and a row with only some of its rows checked shows a dash. The selection holds every checked id. |
| `readonly onLoadChildren?: ((id: string) => Promise<readonly TreeViewNode[]>) \| undefined;` | Loads the children of a row marked `hasChildren` the first time it is expanded. A spinner row shows while the promise is pending and the tree keeps the rows it resolves to; a rejection closes the row so it can be tried again. |
| `readonly onMove?: ((ids: readonly string[], target: TreeViewDropTarget) => void) \| undefined;` | Called when rows are dragged (or moved from the keyboard) to a new place: before or after a row, or inside it. Given, rows can be dragged on web; the tree does not move them itself, so update `items` (`moveTreeNodes` does it). Native has no dragging and ignores it. |

| `readonly palette?: ChartPalette \| undefined;` | `accent` steps (the default) or the `categorical` set, by weight order. |
| `readonly height?: number \| undefined;` | The plot's height in pixels; 260 by default. |
